Um, my appetite is pretty normal, I think. I always make sure to eat so I have enough energy for the site. But my sleep... well, I guess it's been a little all over the place? I hope I'm not complaining too much, it's probably just normal stress. 

For a while there, I felt like I was doing really great. I was getting by on just three or four hours of sleep a night and still showing up totally wired and ready for the job and my classes. I thought I was just being a really dedicated apprentice, you know? But lately... I don't know, I either just can't seem to fall asleep at all, or I'll wake up way before my alarm goes off and feel like I never even rested. It's probably just a normal phase, right? I'm still trying my best to push through it and not let it bother anyone.

I mean, I try not to worry too much about the future. That wouldn't be very helpful, right? I mostly just focus on the day-to-day stuff at the site and making sure I'm doing what I'm supposed to do. Sometimes I might overthink a mistake I made, like I mentioned, but I don't sit around stressing about what's going to happen next year or anything. I think I'm pretty good at taking things one step at a time. Is that what you mean? I hope that's the right answer.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any changes in your appetite or sleep pattern lately?\n\nPatient: Um, my appetite is pretty normal, I think. ========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Um, well... I guess maybe a little bit? But it's nothing too serious, I promise. I just... lately, I can kind of just walk right past my sketchbook without picking it up.
